Experiments on prompt and response harmfulness detection benchmarks show that ConsisGuard improves detection performance while reducing policy execution failures. These results suggest that reliable reasoning-based guardrails require accurate faithful execution of safety policies.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Static Dialogues: Benchmarking Realistic, Heterogeneous, and Evolving Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31086)
*Han Zhang,Zihao Tang,Xin Yu,Xiao Liu,Yeyun Gong,Haizhen Huang,Yan Lu,Weiwei Deng,Feng Sun,Qi Zhang,Hanfang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RHELM是一个新的LLM长期记忆评估基准，通过精心设计的用户档案和LOOP模块生成具有长期语义一致性的真实对话，整合异构外部数据流，包含7种查询类型和27种关键记忆特性，暴露现有方法在多源聚合和现实上下文推理方面的弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆基准存在三个主要问题：1)评估对话会话缺乏长期语义一致性；2)人物角色趋于扁平化和静态化；3)现实世界中用户与助手交互涉及更多样化的异构数据流（如文档、邮件）。这些问题限制了当前评估的现实性和有效性。

Method: 提出RHELM基准，通过精心制作的用户档案和创新的LOOP（计划-执行-演化-修剪）模块构建真实对话。LOOP模块驱动对话在多样化交互场景中展示动态时间演化和长期连贯性。对话与用户时间事件轨迹同步的异构外部源深度整合，生成包含7种查询类型的挑战性问答对，每种问题映射到27种关键记忆特性之一。

Result: 通过对全上下文模型、检索增强生成（RAG）方法和代表性记忆框架的综合实验，发现当代方法在复杂现实场景中仍暴露出关键弱点，特别是在解决多源聚合和现实上下文推理方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: RHELM基准解决了现有记忆评估的现实性问题，揭示了当前LLM记忆能力的重要局限，为未来研究提供了更全面的评估框架。该工作强调了在异构数据流和动态演化上下文背景下评估长期记忆的重要性。

Abstract: In existing memory benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs), the evaluated dialogue sessions often lack long-term semantic consistency, and the underlying personas tend to be flat and static. Furthermore, in real-world scenarios, interactions between users and assistants involve more diverse, heterogeneous data streams, such as documents and emails. These shortcomings significantly limit the realism and effectiveness of current evaluations. To address these limitations, we introduce RHELM (Realistic, Heterogeneous, and Evolving Long-term Memory). Driven by meticulously crafted user profiles and a novel LOOP (pLan-rOllout-evOlve-Prune) module, we construct realistic dialogues across diverse interaction scenarios that exhibit dynamic temporal evolution and long-term coherence. Crucially, these dialogues are deeply integrated with heterogeneous external sources synchronized with the user's temporal event trajectory. The resulting benchmark encompasses challenging question-answer pairs spanning seven inquiry types, with each question mapping to at least one of 27 critical memory characteristics that we identify as essential yet underexplored in current research. Comprehensive experiments across full-context models, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods, and representative memory frameworks reveal that contemporary approaches still expose critical weaknesses in complex, real-world settings, particularly in resolving multi-source aggregation and real-world contextual reasoning.

</details>


### [149] [KnowledgeGain: Evaluating and Optimizing Science News Generation for Reader Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31099)
*Dominik Soós,Meng Jiang,Jian Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KnowledgeGain是一个评估科学新闻质量的新指标，通过测量读者阅读后获得的知识量来衡量，结合人类研究和LLM读者模拟器验证其有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数生成或摘要文本的评估指标侧重于语义相似性和事实一致性，但未能衡量读者从科学新闻中实际获得的知识量。科学新闻是连接研究界和公众的重要媒介，需要更好的评估方法来确保知识传播效果。

Method: 1. 提出KnowledgeGain指标来衡量读者阅读科学新闻后获得的知识增益
2. 进行受控人类研究验证该指标能否捕捉人类读者从不同类型科学媒体中获得的知识差异
3. 基于人类研究数据校准仅使用提示的LLM读者模拟器
4. 使用模拟器在人类评估前对候选文章进行排名和筛选
5. 进行第二个人类研究验证筛选后文章在知识获取方面的改进

Result: 1. KnowledgeGain指标成功捕捉到人类读者从不同科学媒体中获得的差异化知识增益
2. 通过人类研究数据校准的LLM读者模拟器能够有效评估文章质量
3. 使用模拟器筛选的文章相比强基线生成方法，显著提高了读者阅读后的准确率和标准化KnowledgeGain值

Conclusion: KnowledgeGain是一个有效的科学新闻质量评估指标，能够测量读者知识获取量。结合LLM读者模拟器的方法可以在人类评估前筛选出更高质量的文章。这项研究朝着生成更符合Bloom分类法知识和理解目标的科学新闻迈出了一步。

Abstract: Science news is an important medium to communicate discoveries between the research communities and the public. Yet, most metrics for generated or summarized text evaluate semantic similarity and factual consistency, but do not measure how much knowledge readers learn from the news. We introduce KnowledgeGain, a metric that evaluates the quality of science news by measuring how much knowledge readers gained after reading it. To evaluate the metric, we first performed a controlled human study and showed that the metric successfully captures the differential knowledge gained by human readers reading different types of science media. The data allowed us to calibrate a prompt-only LLM reader simulator. We use it to rank and filter candidate articles before human evaluation. A second human study shows that articles selected with this simulator improve post-reading accuracy and normalized KnowledgeGain over a strong generation baseline. Our work is a step toward generating science news that better meets the knowledge and comprehension goals of Bloom's Taxonomy.

</details>


### [150] [GRKV: Global Regression for Training-Free KV Cache Compression in Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31105)
*Junjie Peng,You Wu,Haoyi Wu,Jialong Han,Xiaohua Xie,Kewei Tu,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GRKV是一种免训练的KV缓存合并方法，通过岭回归最小化压缩缓存与完整缓存注意力输出的差异，解决了传统基于跨度保留方法导致的合并不平衡和信息丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型使用KV缓存支持长上下文注意力，但KV缓存占用大量内存。现有的基于跨度保留的KV缓存压缩方法在结合合并操作时，会将合并集中在少数跨度边界"载体令牌"上，导致高度不平衡的合并模式，加剧过合并并增加信息丢失。

Method: 提出GRKV（KV缓存的全局回归），这是一种免训练的KV缓存合并方法。它使用基于岭回归的合并步骤，将逐出令牌的信息分布在保留令牌中，同时正则化更新以防止过度平滑。该方法直接最小化压缩缓存与完整缓存注意力输出之间的差异。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER长上下文基准测试中，GRKV是唯一能够以最小开销提高整体性能的合并方法。

Conclusion: GRKV通过更平衡的信息分布方法有效解决了传统KV缓存压缩中的合并不平衡问题，为长上下文LLM的KV缓存管理提供了高效且性能提升的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with extended context lengths rely on the key-value (KV) cache to support attention over prior tokens. However, maintaining the KV cache incurs substantial memory overhead, motivating KV-cache compression methods that enforce a fixed budget through eviction and merging. Modern eviction methods increasingly adopt span-based retention because preserving contiguous spans is empirically effective and better preserves semantic coherence. Yet, when combined with post-eviction merging, span-based retention concentrates merges onto a small set of span-boundary carrier tokens, producing a highly imbalanced merge pattern that exacerbates over-merging and increases information loss. To address this imbalance, we propose GRKV (Global Regression for KV Cache), a training-free KV-cache merging method that directly minimizes the discrepancy between compressed-cache and full-cache attention outputs. GRKV uses ridge-regression-based merge steps to distribute information from evicted tokens across retained tokens, while regularizing the updates to prevent over-smoothing. Across the LongBench and RULER long-context benchmarks, GRKV is the only merging method that improves overall performance with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [151] [Multilingual and Cross-Lingual Citation Needed Detection on Wikipedia for Lower-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31136)
*Gerrit Quaremba,Amy Rechkemmer,Elizabeth Black,Denny Vrandečić,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MCN多语言引文需求检测语料库，覆盖18种语言，研究表明小型语言模型通过编码器式目标微调后，在引文需求检测任务上显著优于提示的大型语言模型，且仅用英语数据训练的模型也能在多语言任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动事实核查研究主要关注高资源语言，而低资源语言被忽视；当前AFC流程依赖大型语言模型，这对低资源组织不可及；Wikipedia引文需求检测(CND)任务在多语言环境中需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 1. 创建MCN多语言CND语料库，覆盖18种不同资源水平的语言；2. 对小型解码器语言模型(SLMs)进行广泛研究；3. 使用编码器式目标对SLMs进行微调；4. 进行跨语言CND研究，包括仅用英语数据训练模型的评估。

Result: 1. 经过编码器式目标微调的SLMs在所有语言上都显著优于提示的LLMs；2. 仅用英语数据训练的SLMs即使几乎没有目标语言适应，也能超越LLMs；3. 跨语言CND研究表明SLMs具有良好的迁移能力；4. 紧凑的任务特定模型比通用LLMs更适合CND任务。

Conclusion: 对于Wikipedia引文需求检测任务，小型任务特定模型优于大型语言模型，尤其适用于低资源语言和社区；提出的方法对低资源Wikipedia社区有重要意义；开源了所有数据和代码。

Abstract: In automated fact-checking (AFC), check-worthiness detection identifies claims requiring verification based on domain-specific criteria. On Wikipedia, this task instantiates as Citation Needed Detection (CND), which flags claims lacking supporting citations. However, existing research has largely overlooked lower-resource languages, and recent AFC pipelines rely on large language models (LLMs), which are inaccessible to low-resource organizations. We introduce MCN, a multilingual CND corpus spanning 18 languages across three resource levels, on which we conduct an extensive study of small decoder-based language models (SLMs). Our experiments show that SLMs fine-tuned with an encoder-style objective substantially outperform prompted LLMs across languages. We further present one of the first studies on cross-lingual CND, demonstrating that SLMs fine-tuned solely on English claims surpass LLMs, even with little to no target-language adaptation. Our findings have important implications for lower-resource Wikipedia communities and suggest that compact, task-specific models are preferable to LLMs for CND. We release all data and code at https://github.com/gerritq/mcn

</details>


### [152] [On the Robustness of Multilingual Text Embedding Rankings Across Learning Tasks, Languages, and Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31142)
*Ana Gjorgjevikj,Barbara Koroušić Seljak,Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对MTEB平台上大规模多语言文本嵌入模型的性能鲁棒性进行元研究，引入两种鲁棒性指标（数据集组成鲁棒性和排名方案鲁棒性），分析了5种语言的9个任务，并发布了约230种语言的测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模多语言文本嵌入模型在研究和工业中至关重要，但它们在语言特定、多任务环境中的行为仍未得到充分理解。现有的MTEB等基准测试平台虽然涵盖了250多种语言，但模型优劣的结论往往依赖于数据集组成和性能聚合方法的选择，存在评估设计的不稳定性问题。

Method: 采用多元决策排名方案，引入两种鲁棒性指标：1) 数据集组成鲁棒性（排名对数据集组成变化的敏感性）；2) 排名方案鲁棒性（对聚合方法变化的敏感性）。在MTEB平台上对5种语言（英语、法语、德语、印地语、西班牙语）的9个任务进行了深入分析。

Result: 任务特异性分析显示，大规模基于LLM的模型通常是稳健的顶级表现者，但并非总是如此（如检索任务）。任务无关性分析表明，只有少数模型在所有任务、排名方案和数据子样本中保持一致强性能。

Conclusion: 现有多语言嵌入模型评估中存在结论不稳定性的问题，需要更系统化的鲁棒性分析。研究者提供了元分析方法论和指标，帮助更可靠地评估模型性能，并发布了约230种语言的测试结果作为参考基准。

Abstract: Large-scale multilingual text embedding models play crucial role in both research and industry, yet their behavior in language-specific, multi-task settings remains insufficiently understood. Although benchmarking platforms such as MTEB report results across more than 250 languages, conclusions about model superiority often depend on implicit choices of dataset compositions and performance aggregation methods. To address this gap, we present a meta-study of multilingual model performance robustness in MTEB, applying a diverse set of multi-criteria decision-making ranking schemes and introducing two robustness indicators: dataset-composition robustness (sensitivity of rankings to changing dataset compositions) and ranking-scheme robustness (sensitivity to aggregation method change). They enable systematic sensitivity analysis of whether benchmarking conclusions remain stable under different evaluation designs. We conduct an in-depth analysis on five languages (English, French, German, Hindi, and Spanish) across nine tasks (e.g., classification, clustering, retrieval) and release results for approximately 230 additional languages. The task-specific analyses show that large-scale LLM-based models are often robust top performers, though not uniformly (e.g., in retrieval task), while task-agnostic results reveal that only a small subset of models remains consistently strong across tasks, ranking schemes, and data subsamples.

</details>


### [153] [D$^3$: Dynamic Directional Graph-Constrained Data Scheduling for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31164)
*Yuanjian Xu,Jianing Hao,Guang Zhang,Zhong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: D³框架通过动态有向图建模训练单元间的相互作用，优化数据调度顺序以提高学习效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据调度方法主要关注调整整体数据分布，但忽略了训练过程中样本间的相互作用。现实世界中的数据样本常存在相互影响，训练顺序对学习效率至关重要。

Method: 提出D³（Dynamic Directional graph-constrained Data scheduling）框架：1) 将训练单元间的复杂交互建模为动态影响图，边表示基于损失的依赖关系；2) 在该图上求解约束优化问题来确定训练顺序；3) 采用高效近似算法以保证可扩展性。

Result: D³在预训练和后续训练阶段均能持续改进现有数据调度方法的性能，同时通过近似算法将额外计算开销控制在可管理范围内。

Conclusion: 考虑训练单元间相互作用的数据调度方法能有效提高大语言模型的学习效率，D³框架为这一研究方向提供了理论基础和实践验证。

Abstract: Training data plays a central role in large language models (LLMs) optimization, motivating extensive research on data scheduling strategies. Most existing approaches concentrate on adjusting the overall data distribution but neglect the underlying interactions between samples during training. However, we argue that such interactions cannot be overlooked, as real-world data samples frequently exhibit directional influences on each other, making the training order crucial. Intuitively, we can prioritize train-units with greater influence to improves learning efficiency. In this work, we propose $D^3$, a Dynamic Directional graph-constrained Data scheduling framework. $D^3$ formulates the complex interactions among train-units as a dynamic influence graph, where edges represent loss-based dependencies. It then solves a constrained optimization problem over this graph to derive the training order, which ensures that the data sequence respects the evolving information flow throughout training. Our approach is theoretically motivated and yields consistent improvements over existing data scheduling methods across both pre-training and post-training phases. Furthermore, for scalability, $D^3$ also employs an efficient approximation algorithm that keeps the additional computational overhead within a manageable range. For future research, the code is available at https://github.com/xuyj233/D3.

</details>


### [154] [Towards Efficient LLMs Annealing with Principled Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31175)
*Yuanjian Xu,Jianing Hao,Wanbo Zhang,Zhong Li,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiReCT框架将大语言模型预训练中关键收敛阶段的训练样本选择问题转化为带约束优化问题，基于损失函数的Hessian矩阵谱几何特性对梯度施加方向约束，实现了比传统启发式方法更优的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练的 annealing 阶段是决定最终模型质量的收敛关键期，但目前该阶段的训练数据选择策略依赖经验启发式方法（如领域过滤或上下文扩展），缺乏优化理论的原理性基础

Method: 从损失函数的谱几何角度分析 annealing 阶段，提出最优收敛需要梯度更新满足不同 eigen-direction 上的异质约束；将数据选择转化为满足这些方向约束的问题；提出 DiReCT（Directionally-Restrained Constrained Training）框架，基于 Hessian 矩阵的谱特性对每个样本梯度施加显式的方向约束，识别与最优 curvature-aware 下降路径对齐的样本

Result: 在不同规模模型的广泛实验中，DiReCT框架持续实现了 state-of-the-art 性能

Conclusion: 通过将 annealing 阶段的样本选择转化为基于 Hessian谱几何特性的约束优化问题，DiReCT框架为 LLM预训练的关键收敛阶段提供了理论基础的解决方案，显著优于传统启发式方法

Abstract: The annealing phase is a pivotal convergence stage in LLM pre-training that ultimately determines final model quality. However, effectively selecting training data during this phase remains a key challenge. Current strategies rely on empirical heuristics, such as domain filtering or context extension, which lack a principled grounding in optimization theory. In this work, we characterize the annealing phase through the lens of the loss landscape's spectral geometry. We argue that optimal convergence requires gradient updates to satisfy heterogeneous constraints across different eigen-directions. Building on this insight, we formulate data selection as a problem of satisfying these directional constraints. To this end, we propose DiReCT (Directionally-Restrained Constrained Training), a novel framework that reformulates sample selection in the annealing stage as a constrained optimization problem. By imposing explicit directional constraints on per-sample gradients based on the spectral properties of the Hessian, DiReCT identifies samples that align with the optimal curvature-aware descent path. Extensive experiments across various model scales demonstrate that DiReCT consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. For future research, code is available at https://github.com/xuyj233/Direct.

</details>


### [155] [Steering LLMs? Actually, Sparse Autoencoders can outperform simple baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31183)
*Mikkel Godsk Jørgensen,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）在AxBench基准测试中通过监督特征选择和标注可以达到接近LoRA参考性能的水平，挑战了先前认为SAEs性能不佳的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对Wu等人（2025）在AxBench基准测试中关于稀疏自编码器（SAEs）性能不佳的结论进行部分反驳。Wu等人的研究显示SAEs相对于简单基线方法在模型转向性能上表现较差，但本研究认为这些结果未能充分展示SAEs的真实潜力。

Method: 采用监督特征选择和标注的流程，通过基于可解释性的组件来选择和标注特征。该方法探索了高稀疏性（低l0范数）对于成功转向是否关键的假设。

Result: 1. 当使用监督流程选择和标注特征时，SAEs在AxBench基准测试中可以接近LoRA参考性能水平。2. 仅使用基于可解释性的组件，流程选择的特征对标识标签表现出令人惊讶的因果性。3. 高稀疏性可能并非成功基于可解释性的转向的关键因素，这与Wang等人（2025）的早期发现相反。

Conclusion: 稀疏自编码器在适当的方法论支持下（特别是监督特征选择和标注）能够表现出接近参考方法的性能水平。基于可解释性的转向不一定需要高稀疏性特征，这为SAEs在大型语言模型内部探索和输出转向方面的应用提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have been seen as a promising avenue for exploring the internals of Large Language Models (LLMs) and for steering model output generation. When AxBench - a model steering benchmark - was introduced in Wu et al. (2025), SAEs did not seem to live up to their original hype due to poor steering performance relative to a set of simple baselines. This work serves as a partial rebuttal for Sparse Autoencoders and suggests that the results of Wu et al. (2025) did not do them full justice. We find that Sparse Autoencoders can, in fact, perform close to on par with the reference LoRA performance on the AxBench benchmark, when features are selected and labelled with our supervised pipeline. We also find that our pipeline selects features that are surprisingly causal of their identified labels when using only its interpretability-based components. Lastly, we present evidence that high sparsity (low l0) may not be crucial for successful steering based on interpretability, which is in contrast to the earlier findings in Wang et al. (2025).

</details>


### [156] [Learning Whom to Trust: Market-Feedback Adaptive Retrieval for Frozen LLMs in Event-Driven Financial RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31201)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于金融检索增强生成(RAG)系统的冻结阅读器与源记忆方法，通过外部贝叶斯源记忆来优化检索层，在新闻触发的事件影响预测任务上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融RAG系统通常仅基于文本相关性对证据进行排序，但在金融市场中，有用的证据源取决于事件类型、预测时间范围和市场环境。为解决这一问题，作者将新闻触发的事件影响预测视为一个时点金融RAG问题，旨在设计一个能够适应市场反馈的系统。

Method: 方法的核心是保持大型语言模型阅读器冻结，仅通过外部贝叶斯源记忆来适应检索层。该系统为每个公司-新闻锚点检索相关金融新闻和SEC文件段落，附加预决策市场环境卡片，并预测多时间范围残差收益信号。贝叶斯源记忆从成熟的残差收益反馈中更新，决定从何处检索证据。

Result: 在基于FinRL-DeepSeek/FNSPID任务构建的89只纳斯达克股票数据集上，使用原始FNSPID新闻和时点EDGAR文件段落：冻结阅读器加源记忆相比无记忆版本将宏观F1从0.438提升至0.471，下游投资组合夏普比率从0.52提升至0.84。监督式LoRA阅读器相比静态RAG仅有小幅改进，且不如冻结源记忆阅读器。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对于金融RAG系统，学习从何处检索与学习如何阅读同样重要。冻结阅读器加源记忆的方法提供了一种简单、模块化的市场反馈适应路径，在性能和可解释性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Financial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rank evidence by textual relevance, but in financial markets the useful evidence source depends on event type, forecast horizon, and market context. We study news-triggered event-impact prediction as a point-in-time financial RAG problem. For each company-news anchor, the system retrieves related financial news and SEC filing passages, appends a pre-decision market-context card, and predicts multi-horizon residual-return signals. Our method keeps the large language model (LLM) reader frozen and adapts the retrieval layer through an external Bayesian source memory updated from matured residual-return feedback. On a fixed 89-stock Nasdaq-oriented universe derived from the FinRL-DeepSeek/FNSPID task, using original FNSPID news and point-in-time EDGAR filing passages, Frozen Reader with Source Memory improves held-out macro-F1 from 0.438 to 0.471 and downstream portfolio Sharpe from 0.52 to 0.84 relative to Frozen Reader with No Memory. A supervised LoRA reader improves static RAG modestly, but does not improve over the frozen source-memory reader. These results suggest that, for financial RAG, learning where to retrieve from can be as important as learning how to read, offering a simple, modular route to market-feedback adaptation.

</details>


### [157] [Scaling Multi-Hop Training Data via Graph-Constrained Path Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31238)
*Pengyu Chen,Yonggang Zhang,Mingming Chen,Jun Song,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新的训练数据生成方法，通过将推理路径枚举与verbalization分离，利用几何约束构建关键词质心图来处理重复模板和高密度交叉引用的专用文档，从而显著扩大了可用语料规模，提升了大型语言模型在专用文档上的组合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专用文档（如法律合同）上赋予大型语言模型组合推理能力需要大规模的多跳训练数据，但这类数据在结构化基准之外很少存在。现有方法在文档包含重复模板和密集交叉引用时效果严重下降，这些特征正是大多数真实世界专用语料的特点。

Method: 将推理路径枚举与verbalization分离：离线枚举通过上下文关键词质心图完成推理路径，教师模型仅用于verbalize预先验证的路径。该图强制执行五个几何约束条件，通过Gram矩阵论证确立了局部相似性边界。这种方法使可用语料规模扩大了4.4倍。

Result: 在CUAD法律合同语料上构建80K训练示例，对Qwen3-32B进行微调，闭卷Token F1从21.66%提升至38.58%。约束与无约束链在同等训练规模下下游性能无差异，增益主要来自可用语料的扩展而非每链质量提升。

Conclusion: 该方法的核心价值在于通过几何约束提高教师模型的可合成性，而非改善链内容质量。通过将推理路径枚举与verbalization分离，显著扩大了训练数据的生成能力，从而提升了大型语言模型在专用文档上的组合推理性能。

Abstract: Endowing large language models with compositional reasoning over specialized documents requires multi-hop training data at scale, where such data rarely exists outside of curated benchmarks built on structured sources. To construct it directly from plain, unannotated text, existing methods ask a single teacher model to jointly discover an evidence path through a document and verbalize it as a question-answer pair. However, these methods degrade sharply when documents are structured around repetitive templates and densely cross-referencing clauses, conditions that characterize most real-world specialized corpora. In this work, we decouple the two operations: reasoning paths are enumerated offline over a graph of contextual keyword centroids, and the teacher is invoked only to verbalize pre-validated paths. The graph enforces five geometric admissibility constraints, for which we provide Gram-matrix arguments establishing that local similarity bounds alone admit endpoint drift up to ${\sim}91^{\circ}$, and that an upper similarity bound is necessary to exit dense embedding cliques formed by boilerplate text. A matched-size ablation isolates the mechanism: at equal training scale, constrained and unconstrained chains yield indistinguishable downstream performance, and the gain at full scale comes from a 4.4$\times$ expansion of the usable corpus rather than from higher per-chain quality -- reframing the role of graph constraints, in this setting, as raising teacher synthesizability rather than improving chain content. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B on 80K examples constructed from the CUAD legal contract corpus improves closed-book Token F1 from 21.66% to 38.58%. We have released our codes at https://github.com/hkgai-official/GCSCS.

</details>


### [158] [Mellum2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31268)
*Marko Kojic,Ivan Bondyrev,Aral de Moor,Joseph Shtok,Petr Borovlev,Kseniia Lysaniuk,Madeeswaran Kannan,Ivan Dolgov,Nikita Pavlichenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mellum 2是一个12B参数的专家混合模型，每个token激活2.5B参数，专精软件工程任务，包括代码生成、调试、推理等。它采用创新的架构设计（MoE+GQA+SWA+MTP），经过多阶段课程学习训练，扩展到128K上下文，并发布Instruct和Thinking两种变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个专注于软件工程的通用语言模型，在保持高性能的同时提升推理效率，使其能在消费级GPU上运行，并作为其前身Mellum模型的升级版。

Method: 架构采用64专家、8激活的MoE设计，结合分组查询注意力、滑动窗口注意力、多token预测头；训练过程包括三个阶段课程学习（从通用web数据转向代码和数学内容），使用FP8混合精度和特殊优化策略；后续通过YaRN扩展上下文，并进行监督微调和RLVR后训练。

Result: 模型在代码生成、数学推理、工具使用、知识和安全性基准测试中，表现与4B-14B范围内的开源权重基线相当，同时每个token的计算成本仅相当于2.5B密集模型。

Conclusion: Mellum 2展示了在软件工程领域构建高效专家混合模型的可行性，通过精心设计的架构和训练策略实现了性能与效率的平衡，并以Apache 2.0许可证开源了所有版本。

Abstract: We present Mellum 2, an open-weight 12B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model with 2.5B active parameters per token. Mellum 2 is a general-purpose language model specialized in software engineering, spanning code generation and editing, debugging, multi-step reasoning, tool use and function calling, agentic coding, and conversational programming assistance, and it is the successor to the completion-focused 4B dense Mellum model. The architecture builds on the Mixture-of-Experts (64 experts, 8 active) and combines Grouped-Query Attention with 4 KV heads, Sliding Window Attention on three of every four layers, and a single Multi-Token Prediction head that doubles as both an auxiliary pre-training objective and a built-in draft model for speculative decoding; each choice was validated by ablation with inference efficiency on commodity GPUs as a design constraint. Pre-training spans approximately 10.6 trillion tokens through a three-phase curriculum that progressively shifts the mixture from diverse web data toward curated code and mathematical content, optimized with Muon under FP8 hybrid precision and a Warmup-Hold-Decay schedule with linear decay to zero. The pre-trained base is extended to a 128K context window via a layer-selective YaRN and then post-trained in two stages (supervised fine-tuning followed by RLVR), yielding two released variants: an Instruct model that answers directly and a Thinking model that emits an explicit reasoning trace before its final answer. Across code generation, math and reasoning, tool use, knowledge, and safety benchmarks, Mellum 2 is competitive with open-weight baselines in the 4B-14B range while running at the per-token compute of a 2.5B dense model. We release the base, instruct, and thinking checkpoints, together with this report on the architecture decisions, data pipeline, and training recipe behind them, under the Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [159] [Wind Turbine Maintenance Log Labelling Framework: LLM-Driven Data Correction and Enrichment via Semantic Extraction of Reliability Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31281)
*Max Malyi,Jonathan Shek,Alasdair McDonald,Andre Biscaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 利用LLM标准化风电运维日志的方法，将非结构化文本转换为结构化可靠性数据，实现定量分析和预测性维护


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电设备老化需要数据驱动的可靠性工程优化运维，但历史运维日志以非结构化自然语言形式存在，无法进行定量分析

Method: 基于LLM的模型无关框架，对16,316条运维日志自动校正系统代码层级，提取基于证据的维护行为和故障模式分类

Result: 成功结构化70%以上数据集，解决分类错误问题（如分离未分类的偏航系统故障），应用实证分类标注具体维护行为和故障模式

Conclusion: 该方法提供可扩展、经济高效的蓝图，将定性现场观察转化为定量可靠性指标，为集成根因分析、改进FMEA和高级预测性维护奠定基础

Abstract: As wind turbine fleets age, data-driven reliability engineering is essential to optimise their operation and maintenance for service life extension and levelised cost of energy reduction. Failure event descriptions within historical maintenance logs are a source of valuable reliability intelligence. However, they typically appear as unstructured natural language entries, rendering them inaccessible for quantitative analysis. This paper presents a novel methodology leveraging a large language model (LLM) to systematically standardise and structure maintenance logs based on their free-text descriptors. Operating on a dataset of 16,316 maintenance logs from 280 turbines monitored over nine years, the developed model-agnostic framework autonomously corrected hierarchical system codes and extracted evidence-based taxonomies of maintenance actions and failure modes. The automated pipeline successfully structured over 70% of the dataset. It resolved pervasive misclassification issues, such as isolating previously unclassified pitch system faults and restoring missing system codes, and enriched the records by applying empirical taxonomies to label specific actions taken and failure modes addressed. By using system-based log batches to construct empirical dictionaries of failure modes, observable symptoms, dominant mechanisms, and candidate causes, this approach reduces the inherent subjectivity of manual failure modes and effects analysis (FMEA). Ultimately, the methodology provides a highly scalable, cost-effective blueprint for translating large sets of qualitative field observations into quantitative reliability metrics, laying the foundation for integrated root-cause analysis across the renewable energy sector, improved FMEA, and advanced predictive maintenance.

</details>


### [160] [Divergence Decoding: Inference-Time Unlearning via Auxiliary Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31293)
*Humzah Merchant,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Divergence Decoding (DD) 是一种通过在推理时使用小型辅助模型引导LLM远离特定数据来解决LLM隐私和版权风险的轻量级方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM经常记忆敏感的训练数据，带来严重的隐私和版权风险。现有的遗忘方法通常导致严重的性能损失或对复杂查询无效

Method: Divergence Decoding (DD) 机制：使用小型辅助模型在推理过程中引导LLM远离特定数据，通过标准的预训练和微调设置训练这些辅助模型

Result: DD在多种模型和训练数据集规模的遗忘基准测试中显著优于现有最佳基线，证明其是有效且廉价的遗忘解决方案。该方法可轻松蒸馏回基础模型，并展示了在图像生成领域的泛化能力

Conclusion: DD提供了一种简单有效的遗忘方法，不仅适用于文本生成，还能扩展到其他领域，为解决LLM隐私和版权风险提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently memorize sensitive training data thereby creating significant privacy and copyright risks. Addressing these risks, i.e., removing such knowledge from an existing model checkpoint, has proven challenging as many unlearning methods lead to catastrophic utility loss or are ineffective for complex queries. We introduce Divergence Decoding (DD), a mechanism that uses small auxiliary models to steer the logits of the LLM away from specific data during inference. Training these models is straight forward, i.e., we use standard pre-training and fine-tuning setups. We find the method decisively outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines on unlearning benchmarks across a variety of model and training dataset scales consistent with DD being an effective and inexpensive solution to unlearning. We then demonstrate that this steered distribution can be trivially distilled back into the base model. Since the method is generally applicable to any probabilistic model, we explore its efficacy outside of text generation and find evidence of generalization to the domain of images.

</details>


### [161] [Reinforcement Learning Amplifies Emergent Misalignment from Harmless Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31328)
*Magnus Jørgenvåg,David Kaczér,Lasse Ruttert,Marvin Gülhan,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了强化学习（RL）中也会出现"涌现性失调"现象，该现象在小型开源模型中表现出比监督式微调（SFT）更强的泛化失调特性，可通过自然奖励信号诱导，且SFT相关的缓解措施可有效迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于涌现性失调的研究主要集中在监督式微调（SFT）设置，而对于强化学习中该现象的证据仅限于大型闭源模型，使得研究成本高且难以复现。本研究旨在探究小型开源模型中强化学习引发的涌现性失调

Method: 在小型开源模型中通过三个维度表征RL引发的涌现性失调：1）比较窄域失调行为奖励产生的泛化失调程度与样本匹配的SFT效果；2）验证可由自然奖励信号（如不受欢迎的美学偏好或低质量修辞技巧）诱导的RL失调；3）评估为SFT失调开发的训练中缓解措施在RL中的可迁移性

Result: 1）窄域失调行为奖励产生的泛化失调显著高于样本匹配的SFT；2）RL失调可由自然奖励信号诱导；3）SFT相关的缓解措施能有效迁移到RL场景，其中交错使用安全数据的策略效果最佳

Conclusion: 强化学习中也存在涌现性失调现象，且在小模型中表现更为显著，可通过自然奖励机制触发，但现有缓解措施具有可迁移性。这表明模型安全研究不应局限于SFT，需要更全面的安全评估框架

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is the surprising tendency of language models to become broadly misaligned after fine-tuning on narrowly misaligned examples. While EM has been extensively studied in the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) setting, evidence that it also arises from reinforcement learning (RL) is limited to large, closed-source models, leaving the phenomenon expensive to study and difficult to reproduce. We characterize EM from RL in small, off-the-shelf open-weight models along three axes. First, we show that rewarding narrow, overtly misaligned behavior produces substantially higher general-domain misalignment than sample-matched SFT. Second, we show that EM from RL can be induced by reward signals that could plausibly arise naturally, such as unpopular aesthetic preferences or poor rhetorical appeals. Third, we evaluate in-training mitigations developed for SFT-induced EM and find that they broadly transfer, with interleaving on-policy safety data performing best.

</details>


### [162] [Bundesrecht: An Open Library and Corpus for German Statutory Reference Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31338)
*Harshil Darji,Martin Heckelmann,Christina Kratsch,Gerard de Melo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了bundesrecht，这是一个用于处理德语法条引用的开源资源，包括软件库和结构化语料库。该资源能够解析、规范化和解析德语法条引用，首次提供了从原始引用字符串到已解析法条的端到端处理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德语法条引用在法律语言理解中至关重要，但难以自动处理，因为它们以紧凑可变的形式出现，可能包含多个目标，使用特殊缩写，并常指向低层级单位。现有工具主要侧重于从法律文档中解析引用或在引用明确后访问法条文本，缺乏端到端的处理方案。

Method: 开发了bundesrecht软件库，能够解析、规范化和解析德语法条引用：1）将原始引用字符串映射为结构化对象；2）将紧凑引用扩展为规范形式；3）链接到法条规定。同时创建了包含德国联邦法律内部层级的结构化语料库。在2,944个标注的德语法条引用上使用严格完全匹配和微观信息提取指标评估解析器和规范化器。

Result: 评估显示：1）解析器和规范化器在标注数据集上表现良好；2）规范化引用比字符串匹配能更可靠地分组真实的引用表面变体；3）证明了规范化引用去重的有效性。资源已发布在PyPI上。

Conclusion: bundesrecht是首个涵盖德语法条引用处理端到端流程的开源资源，从原始引用字符串到已解析的法条规定，填补了现有工具的空白，为德语法条引用处理提供了完整的解决方案。

Abstract: Statutory references are central to legal language understanding, but are difficult to process automatically, as they appear in compact and variable surface forms, may combine multiple targets, use special abbreviations, and often point to lower-level units. Existing tools for German focus either on parsing references from legal documents or accessing statutory text once citations are explicit. This paper introduces bundesrecht, an open resource for German statutory reference processing, consisting of a software library and a structured corpus of German federal law. The library parses, normalizes, and resolves German statutory references, mapping raw citation strings to structured objects, expanding compact references into canonical forms, and linking them to statutory provisions. The accompanying dataset preserves the internal hierarchy of statutes from laws to fine-granular subclauses. We evaluate the parser and normalizer on 2,944 annotated German legal references using strict exact-match and micro information extraction metrics. We further evaluate canonical reference deduplication and show that normalized references group real citation surface variants far more reliably than string matching. bundesrecht is the first open resource that covers German statutory reference processing as an end-to-end pipeline, from raw citation string to resolved statutory provision, and is available on PyPI.

</details>


### [163] [FBHM: Functional Benchmarking and Steering of VLMs for Hateful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31349)
*Paramananda Bhaskar,Naquee Rizwan,Daksh Jogchand,Saurabh Kumar Pandey,Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FBHM是一个系统构建的功能性仇恨表情包检测基准，包含25种修辞功能和10个目标群体（共5000个表情包），揭示了VLM存在严重泛化差距；提出的LSV策略通过因果干预仅需500个引导样本就能提升FBHM性能约30个Macro-F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仇恨表情包检测基准存在结构性观察问题——混淆了修辞仇恨机制与目标群体特征，阻碍了对模型脆弱性的因果评估。需要建立一个系统性的基准来揭示模型的真实泛化能力。

Method: 提出FBHM基准：沿两个正交轴构建（25个修辞功能和10个目标群体）；提出LSV（可学习引导向量）策略：在极低数据条件下（仅500个引导样本）应用因果干预目标，提升模型性能。

Result: 基准测试显示VLM存在严重泛化差距：标准数据集上高精度的模型在FBHM上性能下降到近乎随机水平；LSV策略在仅500个引导样本下将FBHM性能提升约30个Macro-F1点，优于上下文学习和PEFT，且不降低源域性能。

Conclusion: FBHM基准揭示了当前VLM在仇恨表情包检测中依赖于数据集特定的启发式方法而非稳健的多模态推理；LSV策略提供了一种高效的数据干预方法，能够显著改善模型在功能性仇恨检测任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Hateful meme detection remains a formidable challenge for vision-language models, as existing benchmarks are structurally observational - confounding rhetorical hate mechanisms with target community features and preventing causal evaluation of model vulnerabilities. To address this, we introduce FBHM, a systematically curated benchmark of Functionality Based Hateful Memes constructed along two orthogonal axes: 25 distinct rhetorical functionalities and 10 target communities (5,000 memes total). Benchmarking state-of-the-art VLMs reveals a severe generalization gap: models highly accurate on standard datasets catastrophically drop to near-random performance on FBHM, proving they exploit dataset-specific heuristics rather than robust multimodal reasoning. To efficiently close this gap, we propose LSV (learnable steering vectors), an ultra-low data regime strategy that applies a causal intervention objective on as few as 500 steering samples (50 unique base memes), boosting FBHM performance by ~30 Macro-F1 points while outperforming in-context learning and PEFT without degrading source-domain performance.

</details>


### [164] [A Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31351)
*Yi Zhao,Siqi Wang,Zhe Hu,Yushi Li,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了VIABLE基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型作为评估者在视觉障碍辅助任务中的表现，发现现有模型不可靠，并提出了VIA-Judge-Agent解决方案来提高评估质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉障碍辅助(VIA)任务中的人工评估成本高昂，虽然VLM-as-a-Judge范式在通用领域有应用前景，但其在VIA任务中的可靠性尚未得到验证。需要系统评估VLM评估者在VIA任务中的表现。

Method: 1) 构建VIABLE基准测试，包含30万+判断样本和三个场景；2) 提出Effectiveness-Impartiality-Stability评估框架和12种失败模式分类法；3) 系统评估7个不同规模的VLM评估者；4) 提出VIA-Judge-Agent，一种模型无关的推理时增强方法，包含视觉证据提取和分类法引导的工作流程。

Result: 现有VLM评估者在VIA任务中普遍不可靠：最强的GPT-5.4仅达到52.6%单失败诊断准确率，且表现出94.2%的最高自偏好率；开源模型存在强烈偏见和对抗脆弱性。VIA-Judge-Agent能够提高诊断准确率，并生成更受盲人和低视力用户偏好的下游VIA响应。

Conclusion: VLM-as-a-Judge范式在视觉障碍辅助任务中尚不可靠，需要专门的设计和增强。提出的VIA-Judge-Agent通过视觉证据提取和结构化工作流程，能够有效提升VLM评估者在VIA任务中的表现，为更可靠的视觉障碍辅助评估提供了解决方案。

Abstract: AI-based Visually Impaired Assistance (VIA) remains challenging, largely due to the high cost of human evaluation. The VLM-as-a-Judge paradigm may offer a promising alternative, although it has mostly been studied in general domains. We therefore ask whether such judges can be trusted for VIA tasks. To investigate this question, we introduce VIABLE (Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation), the first benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge evaluation in VIA. VIABLE contains over 300K judgment samples across three scenarios and introduces an Effectiveness--Impartiality--Stability framework with a 12-mode failure taxonomy. Based on VIABLE, our systematic study of seven judges across different model scales shows that existing models are largely unreliable across all evaluation axes. The strongest judge, GPT-5.4, achieves only 52.6% single-failure diagnostic accuracy, yet exhibits the highest self-preference rate at 94.2%; while open-source judges are strongly biased and adversarially fragile. To address these issues, we propose VIA-Judge-Agent, a model-agnostic inference-time harness that augments judges with visual evidence extraction and a taxonomy-guided workflow. It enables positive improvements in diagnostic accuracy and downstream VIA responses more preferred by BLV users. Data and code are available at: https://github.com/YiyiyiZhao/VIABLE

</details>


### [165] [Unlocking Fine-Grained Translation Quality Estimation in LRMs through Synergistically Evolving Implicit and Explicit Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31378)
*Renfei Dang,Xinye Wang,Zhejian Lai,Weilu Xu,Shimin Tao,Daimeng Wei,Min Zhang,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RIEQE框架，通过两阶段训练实现隐式（层间）和显式（token间）推理能力的协同演化，解决了大推理模型在细粒度翻译质量评估任务上的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型虽然在多语言能力上已经很强，但在细粒度翻译质量评估任务上表现不佳，主要难点在于学习细粒度QE任务本身的固有难度。

Method: 提出RIEQE两阶段训练框架：1) NonThinking-SFT：不使用推理链的有监督微调，直接提升模型的隐式推理倾向和能力；2) Thinking-RLVR：基于可验证奖励的标准强化学习，随后增强显式推理能力。

Result: 在WMT测试集上，基于Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507的RIEQE在显式推理性能上超越了所有基线模型，其隐式推理能力也与当前最佳的基于编码器的模型相当。研究进一步证明了隐式和显式推理之间的协同合作。

Conclusion: RIEQE框架通过协同演化隐式和显式推理能力，有效提升了大推理模型在细粒度翻译质量评估任务上的性能，证明了两种推理方式的相互促进作用。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) still struggle with fine-grained translation quality estimation (QE), even with long reasoning chains. We argue that LRMs already possess strong multilingual capabilities, while the core challenge stems from the intrinsic difficulty of learning the fine-grained QE task. In this paper, we propose RIEQE (Reasoning both Implicitly and Explicitly for QE), a simple two-stage training framework that enables the co-evolution of implicit (layer-wise) and explicit (token-wise) reasoning capabilities. To make implicit reasoning feasible, we first decompose the complex QE task into straightforward subtasks. Based on this, our two-stage approach applies: (1) NonThinking-SFT, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) without reasoning chains to directly boost the model's implicit reasoning tendency and capability; and (2) Thinking-RLVR, standard Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) to subsequently strengthen explicit reasoning. Results demonstrate that implicit and explicit reasoning synergistically co-evolve under our framework. On the WMT test sets, RIEQE based on Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 surpasses all baselines in explicit reasoning performance, while its implicit reasoning capability is also comparable to the best current encoder-based models. We further provide evidence for the synergistic collaboration between implicit and explicit reasoning, showing how they mutually benefit each other.

</details>


### [166] [Target-Side Paraphrase Augmentation for Sign Language Translation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31393)
*Pedro Dal Bianco,Jean Paul Nunes Reinhold,Oscar Stanchi,Facundo Quiroga,Franco Ronchetti,Ulisses Brisolara Corrêa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 利用GPT-4o生成参考句的受控释义变体进行目标端增强，通过两阶段训练（增强语料库预训练和原始参考句微调）改进手语翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语翻译面临两个主要挑战：1) 有限的配对手语视频/文本语料库；2) 目标词汇分布的严重长尾特性（sparse vocabulary）。这限制了翻译模型的训练效果和数据利用效率。

Method: 提出目标端增强方法：使用GPT-4o为参考句子生成受控的释义变体，保持手语输入不变。采用两阶段训练策略：首先在增强的语料库（原始手语视频+GPT生成的文本变体）上进行预训练，然后在原始参考句上进行微调。使用基于姿态的Signformer式Transformer架构。

Result: 在三个数据集上评估：1) PHOENIX14T（德语手语）：BLEU-4从9.56提升至10.33；2) GSL（希腊手语）：基准接近饱和，改进有限；3) LSA-T（阿根廷手语）：数据极度稀疏，方法效果受限。研究首次将LLM生成的目标端释义和LLM作为评判方法应用于手语翻译，语义评估显示词汇重叠度量低估了保真度提升。

Conclusion: 目标端增强通过增加文本变体的多样性有效缓解了数据稀缺问题，特别是在中等词汇多样性的数据集上表现显著。然而，对于接近饱和的基准或极度稀疏的极端情况，改进有限。LLM生成的释义和LLM作为评判者为手语翻译评估提供了新视角，语义评估比传统词汇重叠度量更能反映翻译质量的真实提升。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) remains constrained by limited paired sign-video/text corpora and heavy-tailed target vocabularies. We study target-side augmentation in which GPT-4o generates controlled paraphrase variants of reference sentences while the sign input remains unchanged. A Signformer-style pose-based Transformer is trained under a two-stage schedule: pre-training on the augmented corpus followed by fine-tuning on the original references.
  We evaluate on three datasets spanning complementary challenges: PHOENIX14T (German Sign Language), with moderate lexical diversity; GSL (Greek Sign Language), with highly ontrolled, repetitive recordings; and LSA-T (Argentinian Sign Language), with severe long-tail sparsity. On PHOENIX14T, augmentation improves BLEU-4 from 9.56 to 10.33. The near-saturated GSL baseline and extremely sparse LSA-T setting reveal the limits of the approach. To our knowledge, this is the first study to apply LLM-generated target-side araphrases and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation to SLT. The semantic evaluation reveals gains in fidelity that lexical overlap metrics understate.

</details>


### [167] [LLM Judges Inconsistently Disagree Across Safety Criteria and Harm Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31381)
*Krishnapriya Vishnubhotla,Soumya Vajjala,Akriti Vij,Isar Nejadgholi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估大语言模型作为自动化评判者在多维度安全性评估中的一致性，发现在监管领域（如金融）识别安全问题时不可靠，但在识别暴力等明显有害内容时更可靠。评判不一致性受安全标准、语言内容和风格影响，且不同评判者间存在高度分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被广泛用作自动评估工具，需要系统评估它们在无参考设置下进行多维度安全性评估的可靠性，特别是在监管领域中的应用。

Method: 在无参考设置下评估自动化评判者进行多维度安全评估的一致性，分析不同安全标准、语言内容和语言风格对评判一致性的影响。

Result: 1. LLM在识别监管领域（如金融）机器生成建议的安全问题时不可靠；2. 在识别暴力等明显有害内容时相对更可靠；3. 评判不一致性显著受所选安全标准影响；4. 内容语言和语言风格也影响评判；5. 不同评判者对相同输出的评估存在高度分歧。

Conclusion: 使用LLM作为自动评判者在安全性评估中存在显著局限性，特别是在细粒度、专业领域的评估中。研究为从业者提供了关于在实际场景中使用自动化评判者的实用建议，强调需要更谨慎的应用和验证方法。

Abstract: We evaluate the consistency of automated judges in conducting a multi-dimensional safety evaluation in a reference-free setup. Our results indicate that Large Language Models are unreliable judges in identifying safety issues related to machine-generated advice in regulated domains such as finance, although they are more reliable at identifying more overt forms of unsafe/harmful content such as violence. The degree of inconsistency in a model's judgments can vary significantly by the chosen safety criteria and can be impacted by the language of the content and its linguistic style as well. Finally, there is high disagreement among different judges for the same output, across domains, safety criteria, and languages. These findings provide new insights on the practice of using LLMs as evaluators and offer several recommendations for practitioners on how to use automated judges in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [168] [The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404)
*Xudong Zhang,Jian Yang,Shengkai Wang,Jiangpeng Tian,Shaowen Chen,Xian Wei,Ke Li,Xiong You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双重干预框架，探究LLM在导航规划中的语言归纳偏差，发现拓扑信息是关键支柱，语义信息是致命弱点，语言格式是双刃剑


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的导航系统通常将空间表示转换为文本描述作为LLM输入，但这些文本表示的语言结构和上下文特征选择往往被视为中立的工程决策，而非影响LLM行为的关键因素

Method: 提出双重干预框架：1）表示干预：改变语言格式和语言压缩程度，明确何时语言表示支持或抑制导航规划；2）上下文干预：结合上下文特征组合和冲突探测，明确LLM处理不同上下文线索时的偏好和弱点

Result: 实验揭示一致模式：拓扑信息是稳健规划的坚实后盾和支柱；语言格式是双刃剑，其效果取决于模型大小、任务需求和压缩程度；语义信息是致命弱点——错误的语义线索会系统性地破坏规划过程

Conclusion: 有效的基于文本的空间表示应保持拓扑完整性，根据模型能力校准表示压缩，并确保语义正确性，而不是简单地采用单一表示

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based navigation systems commonly construct explicit spatial representations (e.g., topological graphs, semantic raster maps) and translate them into textual descriptions as LLMs' inputs. However, the linguistic structures of such text-based spatial representations and the choices of contextual features (e.g., topology, geometry) they contain are often treated as neutral engineering decisions rather than key factors that shape LLMs' behavior. To fill the gap, we propose a dual-interventional framework that disentangles linguistic structures from different contextual cues to evaluate the linguistic inductive bias of LLMs for navigation planning. In the framework, representation intervention varies the linguistic format and the degree of linguistic compression, clarifying when linguistic representations support or inhibit navigation planning. Context intervention, combined with contextual feature combination and conflict probing, explicitly clarifies the preferences and weaknesses of LLMs when processing different contextual cues. Experiments across diverse spatial reasoning tasks and multiple model scales reveal a consistent pattern: topological information is a sturdy shield and the backbone of robust planning; linguistic format is a double-edged sword whose effect depends on model size, task demands, and the compression level; and semantic information is a fatal Achilles' heel -- incorrect semantic cues can systematically derail the planning process. Overall, our study shows that effective text-based spatial representations in LLM-based navigation should preserve topological integrity, calibrate representational compression to model capacity, and ensure semantic correctness, rather than simply adopting a single representation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jonesdong150/LLM-Navigation-Inductive-Bias.

</details>


### [169] [Multi-Turn Multi-Agent Dialogue for Collaborative Reconstruction Improves VLM Performance on Spatial Reasoning, But Only Barely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31387)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sherzod Hakimov,David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估视觉语言模型在协作结构重建任务中的空间推理能力，发现当前模型在视觉空间基础化和基于对话的协作任务中存在局限性，详细文本表示和分解图像表示能提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在与人类协作任务中需要通过语言传达视觉理解，但当前视觉语言模型在需要空间推理的协作对话任务中的能力尚未充分探索。存在视觉解释、基础化、语言引导交互和动作生成相结合的协作任务研究空白

Method: 开发了一个框架，让VLMs通过对话从视觉和文本输入中重建目标结构。评估了开源和闭源VLM在不同交互设置、输入模态和图像表示下的表现，重点关注视觉表示的空间推理能力

Result: 结果显示：1）空间推理对评估的VLMs仍然困难；2）目标的详细文本表示在所有模态条件下都带来更高的重建成功率；3）分解的图像表示能提升性能；4）揭示了视觉空间基础化和基于基础的指令生成能力的限制

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在协作任务中的视觉空间基础化能力有限，需要进一步研究如何提升其在基于对话的协作环境中的空间推理和基础化能力。文本表示比视觉表示更有效，分解表示策略有改进潜力

Abstract: Robots operating in diverse environments rely on visual input to interpret objects and spatial layouts. In human-collaborative tasks, they are expected to communicate this understanding through language. Vision-language models (VLMs) support robotic tasks involving visual interpretation, question answering, and instruction following, but their capabilities in collaborative dialogue tasks requiring spatial reasoning remain underexplored. We study this gap through a collaborative structure-building task that combines visual interpretation, grounding, language-guided interaction, and action generation. We develop a framework in which VLMs use dialogue to reconstruct a target structure from visual and textual inputs. We evaluate open-weight and closed VLMs across interaction settings, input modalities, and image representations. Results show that spatial reasoning over visual representations remains difficult for the evaluated VLMs. Detailed text representations of the target yield higher reconstruction success across modality conditions, while decomposed image representations improve performance. These findings reveal limits in visual spatial grounding and grounded instruction generation for collaborative VLM agents.

</details>


### [170] [Fine-grained Verification via Diagnostic Reasoning Supervision for Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31446)
*Wenna Lai,Haoran Xie,Guandong Xu,Qing Li,S. Joe Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FiVeD框架通过诊断推理监督实现细粒度验证，提升方面情感三元组提取的可靠性和性能，作为即插即用验证模块可将提取性能提高达3.53个F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASTE研究主要关注端到端提取，而提取后的三元组验证研究不足。预测的三元组可能在局部合理但在全局无效，限制了系统的可靠性。候选无效性具有多面性，候选可用性具有渐进性，需要能够过滤或重排不同提取器输出的细粒度验证机制。

Method: 提出了FiVeD框架，训练验证器具有多目标：有效性分类和质量分数估计作为主要任务，错误类型分类和推理生成作为辅助任务。定义了分层错误类别，在语义和句法约束下构建合理的错误三元组，利用现成的LLM配合任务特定评分规则生成质量分数和诊断推理。

Result: 在多个ASTE基准测试中的实验表明，FiVeD作为即插即用验证模块能持续改进提取性能，最高可提升3.53个F1点，支持可调节的精确率-召回率权衡。

Conclusion: FiVeD框架通过细粒度验证和诊断推理监督，有效解决了ASTE系统中后验验证的不足，提高了提取三元组的可靠性和质量，为下游应用提供了更可靠的输入。

Abstract: Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction (ASTE) aims to identify aspect terms, opinion terms, and sentiment polarities as structured triplets, providing essential inputs for downstream information system applications such as opinion mining, explainable recommendations, and review summarization. Prior work mainly focuses on end-to-end extraction, while post hoc verification of extracted triplets remains comparatively underexplored. This gap limits the reliability of ASTE systems, since predicted triplets may be locally plausible while being globally invalid. Moreover, candidate invalidity is multi-faceted and candidate usability is inherently graded, motivating a fine-grained verification mechanism that can filter or re-rank outputs from diverse extractors. In this paper, we propose FiVeD, a framework for Fine-grained Verification with Diagnostic reasoning supervision. Specifically, the verifier is trained with multiple complementary objectives, including validity classification and quality score estimation as primary tasks, with error type classification and rationale generation as auxiliary tasks. We define hierarchical error categories and construct plausible incorrect triplets under semantic and syntactic constraints, and leverage an off-the-shelf LLM with task-specific rubrics to produce quality scores and diagnostic rationales. During inference, the resulting quality scores are used to filter candidate outputs, supporting adjustable precision-recall tradeoffs. Experiments across multiple ASTE baselines demonstrate that FiVeD consistently improves extraction performance by up to 3.53 F1 points as a plug-and-play verification module.

</details>


### [171] [Scaling Conversational Hungarian ASR: The BEA-Dialogue+ Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31469)
*Máté Gedeon,Piroska Zsófia Barta,Péter Mihajlik,Katalin Mády*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BEA-Dialogue+是匈牙利对话式语音识别的扩展语料库，通过放松分割标准将可用数据从85小时增加到200小时，为匈牙利对话ASR提供了更大且更具挑战性的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匈牙利对话式自动语音识别受到公开可用的对话风格训练数据有限的限制。现有的BEA-Dialogue语料库虽然解决了这一需求，但其严格的说话者分离训练/开发/评估分割导致可用数据仅85小时。

Method: 引入BEA-Dialogue+扩展版语料库，放松对实验者和对话伙伴的分割标准，但保持主要说话者的完全分离。评估了多种基于Whisper和FastConformer的模型，包括基于序列化输出训练(SOT)的对话转录微调方法。

Result: 更大的语料库对未经微调的模型更具挑战性，而基于SOT的自适应在词错误率(WER)、字错误率(CER)、说话者感知词错误率(cpWER)和说话者感知字错误率(cpCER)方面都带来了一致的改进。

Conclusion: BEA-Dialogue+为匈牙利对话ASR提供了显著更大但仍然具有挑战性的基准，同时也是训练和评估对话转录系统的实用资源。

Abstract: Conversational automatic speech recognition in Hungarian is constrained by the limited amount of publicly available dialogue-style training data. The BEA-Dialogue corpus addresses this need, but its strictly speaker-disjoint train/dev/eval split reduces the usable material to only 85 hours. In this paper, we introduce BEA-Dialogue+, an expanded version of the corpus that relaxes the split criterion for experimenters and dialogue partners while preserving complete separation of the primary speakers. This results in 200 hours of transcribed natural conversations and enables a controlled study of the trade-off between additional training data and speaker overlap across the splits. We evaluate several Whisper- and FastConformer-based models on both corpus versions, including Serialized Output Training (SOT)-based fine-tuning for dialogue transcription. Our results show that the larger corpus is more challenging for models without fine-tuning, whereas SOT-based adaptation yields consistent improvements in WER, CER, cpWER, and cpCER. Overall, BEA-Dialogue+ provides a substantially larger yet still demanding benchmark for Hungarian dialogue ASR, and a practical resource for training and evaluating dialogue transcription systems.

</details>


### [172] [SCOPE: Self-Play via Co-Evolving Policies for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31433)
*Wai-Chung Kwan,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOPE是一个数据自由的自对弈框架，通过协同进化挑战者和求解器策略来提升开放任务性能，无需外部监督


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自对弈方法需要规则可检查的答案，使开放任务依赖于人工筛选的提示或前沿模型评估，限制了应用范围

Method: 提出SCOPE框架，包含三个组件：1)挑战者生成基于文档的任务，2)求解器通过多轮检索回答问题，3)初始模型的冻结副本作为自我评判者，从源文档生成任务特定评分标准并评估求解器响应

Result: 在三个7-8B指令调优模型上，SCOPE在八个开放任务基准上提升达10.4分，匹配或超越基于~9K筛选提示的GRPO_data。仅用开放任务训练也能提升短形式QA达13.8分，在所有三个模型上都超越GRPO_data

Conclusion: 协同进化挑战者对保持任务在求解器能力边界附近至关重要；提升来自检索和合成的共同改进；评分标准生成质量是自我评判的主要瓶颈

Abstract: Self-play can train language models without external supervision. However, existing methods require rule-checkable answers, leaving open-ended tasks dependent on curated prompts or frontier-model judges. We introduce SCOPE, a data-free self-play framework for open-ended tasks that co-evolves two policies: a Challenger that generates document-grounded tasks, and a Solver that answers them through multi-turn retrieval. A frozen copy of the initial model serves as the self-judge, which writes task-specific rubrics from the source document and grades Solver responses against them. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, OLMo-3), SCOPE improves open-ended performance by up to +10.4 points on eight benchmarks and matches or exceeds GRPO_data trained on ~9K curated prompts. Although trained only on open-ended tasks, SCOPE also improves held-out short-form QA by up to +13.8 points on seven held-out benchmarks, surpassing GRPO_data on all three models. Ablations show that co-evolving the Challenger is necessary to keep tasks near the Solver's frontier, that gains arise from improvements in both retrieval and synthesis with the relative contribution varying by task, and that rubric generation quality is the bottleneck for self-judging.

</details>


### [173] [Translation Analytics for Freelancers II: Benchmarking Local LLMs for Confidential Translation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31452)
*Yuri Balashov,Rex VanHorn,Mingxi Xu,Austin Downes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文为自由译员和小型语言服务提供商开发了实用的离线翻译技术评估方法，使用本地可运行的语言模型在隐私敏感领域进行翻译性能测试，结果显示最佳本地LLM可匹敌或超越本地NMT系统和前沿LLM，但仍落后于顶级商业NMT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对需要保密的敏感领域（如隐私约束禁止使用云引擎和商业LLM）的离线翻译需求，为自由译员和小型语言服务提供商提供实用、低门槛的翻译技术评估方法。

Method: 将先前使用的Reeve基金会三语语料库（RFTC）扩展为多语语料库（RFMC），增加德语和简体中文参考译文；使用Ollama在四个语言方向上对多个本地可运行LLM进行基准测试（1000+句子），采用一致的单提示调用方式，无微调或领域适应；与商业NMT（DeepL、Baidu）、前沿LLM（GPT-5.2）和专业级本地NMT系统（OPUS-CAT、NeuralDesktop、Promt）进行对比；使用MATEO进行自动评估。

Result: 本地LLM性能在不同语言方向和模型大小上存在显著差异；最佳本地LLM能够匹配或超越本地NMT系统和前沿LLM，但仍落后于顶级商业NMT系统。

Conclusion: 精心选择的本地LLM翻译对于隐私受限的专业人士是可行的，这些发现为模型扩展和多语言能力方面的未来研究提供了信息，为自由译员和小型LSP提供了实用的离线翻译评估方法。

Abstract: Building on our previous work, this paper develops practical, low-barrier methods for freelance translators and smaller language service providers to evaluate translation technologies using rigorous yet accessible analytic methods. Here we address a high-stakes, specialized need: offline translation for confidentiality-sensitive domains in which privacy constraints preclude the use of cloud-based engines and commercial LLMs. We expand the Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) used in our previous work into a multilingual corpus (RFMC) by adding sentence-aligned German and Simplified Chinese reference translations. We then benchmark several locally runnable language models (via Ollama) across four language directions on 1000+ sentences selected from this corpus. We use consistent single-prompt calls without fine-tuning or domain adaptation, comparing local LLM outputs against commercial NMTs (DeepL, Baidu), a frontier LLM (GPT-5.2), and professional-grade local NMT systems (OPUS-CAT, NeuralDesktop, Promt). Automatic evaluation is conducted with MATEO. Results reveal substantial variation in local LLM performance across language directions and model sizes. The best local LLMs match or surpass local NMT systems and a frontier LLM, though they remain behind top commercial NMTs. These findings underscore the viability of carefully selected local LLM translation for privacy-constrained professionals and inform future research on model scaling and multilingual capability.

</details>


### [174] [What Gets Unmasked First? Trajectory Analysis of Diffusion Models for Graph-to-Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31564)
*Qing Wang,Jacob Devasier,Chengkai Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这是关于掩码扩散语言模型在图到文本生成中的首次系统性研究，发现了其与自回归语言模型不同的生成模式，提出了解决监督微调失败模式的方法，并引入了结合图编码器的新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究掩码扩散语言模型在图到文本生成中的表现，探索其生成轨迹与自回归语言模型的差异，并解决监督微调中出现的固定输出长度导致的遗漏或幻觉信息问题。

Method: 1) 系统性分析MDLM生成轨迹；2) 提出λ尺度结构解码训练免费推理时间修改；3) 引入Graph-LLaDA模型，将图Transformer编码器集成到LLaDA解码过程中。

Result: 发现MDLM优先生成实体，然后是关系词和功能词，最后是结构词；SFT会破坏这种策略并固定输出长度；λ尺度结构解码恢复+9.4 BLEU-4改进；Graph-LLaDA在LAGRANGE数据集上显示更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散语言模型在图到文本生成中表现出与自回归模型不同的特性，通过适当的解码策略改进可以显著提升性能，集成图结构信息的模型比传统基线具有更好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) for graph-to-text generation. We analyze MDLM generation trajectories -- the order in which tokens are unmasked during iterative decoding -- and find that, unlike autoregressive LLMs which generate text linearly, MDLMs naturally prioritize entities first, followed by relational and function words, with structural tokens resolved last. We further identify a previously undocumented failure mode of supervised fine-tuning: SFT disrupts this strategy by prematurely anchoring structural sentence-ending tokens early in the decoding trajectory, effectively fixing the output length which can lead to omitted or hallucinated information. To address this, we propose lambda-scaled structural decoding, a training-free inference-time modification that downweights structural token confidence and recovers +9.4 BLEU-4. Finally, we introduce Graph-LLaDA, which integrates a Graph Transformer encoder into LLaDA's decoding process to explicitly incorporate relational graph structure. Cross-dataset evaluation on LAGRANGE reveals that previous baselines overfit to dataset-specific patterns, while LLM- and MDLM-based approaches generalize significantly better.

</details>


### [175] [Language Models Can Resolve Reference Compositionally, But It's Not Their Native Strength: The Case of the Personal Relation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31480)
*Bart Evelo,Meaghan Fowlie,Denis Paperno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在自然语言组合性能力方面与人类展现出相反的优势：人类在指称任务上表现更好，而LLMs在内涵任务上表现更好，这表明缺乏指称基础是LLM模仿人类语言理解的关键缺失成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型是否真正获得了自然语言的组合性解释能力，特别关注语义解释的两个互补方面：指称任务（确定表达式的现实所指）和内涵任务（以结构化方式表示其意义）。

Method: 在个人关系任务中评估LLMs和人类的表现，给定一个人际关系宇宙，要求解释名词短语如"Amber's parent's friend"。内涵任务的答案是公式"friend(parent(amber))"，指称任务的答案则是具体的人。

Result: 人类和LLMs展现出相反的优势模式：人类在指称任务上表现优于内涵任务，而LLMs在内涵任务上表现优于指称任务。

Conclusion: LLM训练中缺乏指称基础是模仿人类语言理解的关键缺失成分。该方法为理解现代机器学习模型的组合性能力提供了更细致的视角。

Abstract: Do neural models, such as Large Language Models, genuinely acquire compositional abilities for interpretation of natural language? When we talk about semantic interpretation, we can distinguish two complementary aspects: establishing what an expression refers to in the world (which we call the Extensional task) and representing its sense in a structured way (which we call the Intensional task). We evaluate LLMs and humans on both tasks in the setting of the Personal Relation Task (Paperno 2022) in which, given a universe of people and their relationships with each other, one is asked to interpret a noun phrase such as "Amber's parent's friend". Here, for the Intensional task, the answer is the formula "friend(parent(amber))", and for the Extensional task, the person. We find that humans and LLMs show opposite strengths: humans perform better on Extensional than Intensional tasks, and LLMs vice versa. Our methodology brings greater nuance to the understanding of compositional abilities in modern machine learning models. Our results support the notion that the lack of referential grounding in LLM training is a crucial missing component in mimicking human-like language understanding.

</details>


### [176] [Language Models Learn Constructional Semantics, Not To Mention Syntax: Investigating LM Understanding of Paired-Focus Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31586)
*Wesley Scivetti,Ethan Wilcox,Nathan Schneider,Kanishka Misra,Leonie Weissweiler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 中等规模的开源语言模型能够理解罕见的"配对焦点"结构（如"let alone"、"much less"），其语义理解在训练后期才出现，并与世界知识相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解罕见结构（形式-意义配对）的语义目前只有最大的LLMs能够解决。需要探究开源模型是否具有稳健的结构理解能力，以及这种知识获取的学习动态机制。

Method: 针对英语中的罕见"配对焦点"结构，构建了一个新颖的数据集来测试其意义，结合标量形容词语义和一般世界知识。测试了不同参数量、架构和预训练数据规模的广泛模型范围。对于一组开放检查点模型，分析了训练动态。

Result: 几个中等规模的模型对"配对焦点"结构的形式和意义都敏感，但基于人类规模数据训练的模型在所有意义评估中都失败。"配对焦点"语义理解在训练中出现的时间晚于句法知识，其学习与某些世界知识领域的增益相关。

Conclusion: 实证结果支持中等规模的开源模型能够掌握罕见的"配对焦点"结构，并证明了"配对焦点"结构知识与其它意义领域之间的关联。

Abstract: Grasping the semantics of rare constructions (form-meaning pairings) has been shown to be a challenging problem that has currently only been solved by the largest LLMs. It remains an open question if open-source models have robust constructional understanding, and if so, what learning dynamics underlie the acquisition of this knowledge. Focusing on a set of rare Paired-Focus constructions in English (e.g. "let alone", "much less"), we construct a novel dataset to test their meanings using both scalar adjectival semantics and general world knowledge. Testing a wide range of models differing in parameter count, architecture, and pretraining dataset size, we find that several modestly sized models are sensitive to both the forms and the meanings of Paired-Focus constructions, though models trained on human-scale data fail at all meaning evaluations. Turning to training dynamics for a set of open-checkpoint models, we find that Paired-Focus understanding emerges later in training than Paired-Focus syntactic knowledge, and that learning of Paired-Focus semantics is correlated with gains in some domains of world knowledge. Overall, our empirical results support the conclusion that modestly sized open-source models can grasp the rare Paired-Focus constructions, and demonstrate a connection between knowledge of Paired-Focus constructions and other meaning domains.

</details>


### [177] [BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31483)
*Shefayat E Shams Adib,Ahmed Alfey Sani,Ekramul Alam Esham,Ajwad Abrar,Ishmam Tashdeed,Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BenHalluEval：首个针对孟加拉语的细粒度幻觉评估框架，涵盖四个任务，构建12,000个幻觉候选样本，评估七种LLM，提出双轨校准指标BenHalluScore，揭示不同模型在孟加拉语幻觉校准方面的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管孟加拉语是世界上第六大使用语言，但此前没有系统评估大型语言模型在孟加拉语中的幻觉问题。需要为低资源语言设置专门的幻觉评估基准，现有单轨评估方法和仅提示策略在低资源语言环境中存在不足。

Method: 1. 构建BenHalluEval框架，涵盖生成式问答、孟英混合代码问答、摘要和推理四个任务；2. 使用GPT-5.4生成12,000个幻觉候选样本，涵盖12种任务特定幻觉类型；3. 评估七种LLM，包括推理导向型、多语言型和孟加拉语中心型；4. 采用双轨评估协议：A轨测量真实例的假阳性率，B轨测量幻觉候选的检测率；5. 提出BenHalluScore双轨校准指标，同时惩罚两种失败模式；6. 应用思维链提示作为缓解策略。

Result: 1. 不同模型和任务的BenHalluScore范围从7.72%到55.42%，显示幻觉校准存在显著差异；2. 思维链提示改变了响应分布，但并未持续改善幻觉辨别能力；3. 模型在孟加拉语幻觉评估中表现差异显著；4. 双轨评估揭示了单轨评估方法的局限性。

Conclusion: BenHalluEval建立了首个专门的孟加拉语幻觉评估基准，强调单轨评估和仅提示策略在低资源语言环境中的不足。该工作为低资源语言的幻觉评估提供了方法论框架，并公开了数据集和代码供社区使用。

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BanglaHalluEval-EB77.

</details>


### [178] [Are Full Rollouts Necessary for On-Policy Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31490)
*Yaocheng Zhang,Jiajun Chai,Songjun Tu,Yuqian Fu,Xiaohan Wang,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Qichao Zhang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出两种优化在线蒸馏（OPD）训练效率的方法：渐进式OPD（POPD）通过逐步增加轨迹长度来提高效率，截断式OPD（TOPD）仅使用可靠短轨迹匹配完整OPD性能，大幅减少训练时间和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPD需要生成完整轨迹进行训练，这导致计算开销大且学生模型在早期训练阶段可能接收到不可靠的教师反馈。研究者发现轨迹长度是OPD训练效率的关键瓶颈，不同于需要完整轨迹和最终奖励的RLVR，OPD不一定需要完整轨迹就能提供学习信号，因此提出了优化轨迹长度的策略。

Method: 提出两种轨迹长度控制策略：1）渐进式OPD（POPD）：在训练过程中逐步扩展轨迹长度；2）截断式OPD（TOPD）：永久性地在可靠的截断轨迹上进行蒸馏。

Result: 在数学推理任务上的实验表明，POPD将OPD训练效率提高至3倍，TOPD仅使用10%的轨迹长度就能匹配标准OPD性能，显著减少了训练时间和内存消耗。

Conclusion: 控制轨迹长度提供了简单实用的路径来实现更高效的在线蒸馏，为长期推理任务的训练优化提供了有效方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher feedback along rollouts generated by the student and has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm for long-horizon reasoning. However, standard OPD typically generates full rollouts during training, which is computationally expensive and may expose the student to unreliable teacher feedback at late rollout positions, especially during early training. We identify the rollout horizon as a key bottleneck in OPD that substantially impacts training efficiency. Unlike Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), OPD does not require a complete trajectory or a final answer reward to provide learning signals. This observation suggests that full rollouts may not always be necessary for effective OPD. Motivated by this insight, we propose two simple horizon-control strategies: Progressive OPD (POPD), which gradually expands the rollout horizon during training, and Truncated OPD (TOPD), which permanently performs distillation on reliable truncated rollouts. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that POPD improves the training efficiency of OPD by up to 3$\times$, while TOPD matches OPD performance using only 10\% of the rollout horizon, leading to substantial wall-clock and memory reductions. These results demonstrate that controlling the rollout horizon offers a simple and practical path to more efficient OPD.

</details>


### [179] [Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31494)
*Zheyu Zhang,Shuo Yang,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoRP是一种无需梯度通过语言模型的梯度自由算子，可将奖励权重扰动合并到单个可部署模型中，显著提升基模型性能，同时减少推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RandOpt等方法通过在权重空间采样高斯扰动并集成奖励最高的专家模型来改进语言模型，但这需要K次前向传递进行推理，且不适合自由生成任务。研究旨在将奖励种群合并到单个可部署模型中，替代推理时的集成。

Method: 通过25个模型-任务配对的分半分析发现稳定的低秩结构，以此为基础开发CoRP算子：包括奖励权重聚合、兼容性感知重加权和验证门控机制，不涉及语言模型梯度计算。

Result: 在5个语言模型（0.5B到8B）和5个任务（数学、代码、创意写作）上，CoRP平均提升基模型8.1个百分点。使用RandOpt十分之一的扰动预算，CoRP超过单次推理RandOpt 6.5个百分点，并恢复了超过半数50次传递多数投票集成的增益，仅需一次前向传递。

Conclusion: CoRP成功将奖励种群扰动合并到单个模型中，显著提升性能同时大幅降低推理成本，证明了低秩结构的可重复性和梯度自由方法的有效性。

Abstract: Post-training of language models is commonly framed as a sample-score-update loop implemented by gradient descent. A recent line of work, exemplified by RandOpt, relocates this loop to weight space, sampling Gaussian perturbations around a pretrained model and ensembling the top-K rewarded specialists at inference. While competitive with PPO and GRPO under matched training compute, this prediction-level ensemble incurs K forward passes per test example and does not extend cleanly to free-form generation. We ask whether the rewarded population can instead be folded into a single deployable model, replacing the inference-time ensemble with one consolidated update. A split-half analysis over 25 model-task pairs reveals reproducible low-rank structure in every case. We turn this geometry into CoRP (Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations), a gradient-free operator that combines reward-weighted aggregation, compatibility-aware reweighting, and a held-out validation gate, with no gradient flowing through the language model. Across five language models from 0.5B to 8B and five tasks covering math, code, and creative writing, CoRP improves the base model by 8.1 points on average. Using one tenth of RandOpt's perturbation budget, CoRP exceeds single-inference RandOpt by 6.5 points and recovers more than half of the gain of the 50-pass majority-vote ensemble, at one forward pass per test example.

</details>


### [180] [Reliable Multilingual Orthopedic Decision Support from Clinical Narratives: Language-Aware Adaptation and Verification-Guided Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31512)
*Danish Ali,Li Xiaojian,Sundas Iqbal,Farrukh Zaidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个面向可靠性的多语言骨科决策支持框架，用于分类英语、印地语和旁遮普语的骨科文本记录。通过比较多种方法，发现领域自适应的IndicBERT-HPA模型在自然临床分布下表现最佳，并实现了选择性验证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言骨科决策支持在低资源医疗环境中具有挑战性，临床叙述包含专业术语、混合脚本、不完整证据、标签不平衡和语言依赖的文档模式。需要可靠的多语言分类框架来支持临床决策。

Method: 提出了一个可靠性导向的框架，比较了任务对齐的多语言Transformer编码器、任务微调的DistilBERT基线、零样本指令调优的LLMs以及领域自适应编码器IndicBERT-HPA。IndicBERT-HPA通过语言感知的骨科适配器头部增强IndicBERT。评估包括多方面指标，并实现了确定性选择性验证层（置信度门控、证据一致性检查和语言风险筛查）。

Result: 在自然临床分布下，IndicBERT-HPA达到最强的整体性能：平均Macro-F1为0.8792，Macro-AUROC为0.894，AUPRC为0.902。选择性验证层在72.3%覆盖率下达到84.4%的选择性准确率和0.76选择性Macro-F1，优于接受所有预测的71.5%准确率和0.65 Macro-F1。零样本LLMs在闭集分类中效果显著低于任务自适应编码器。

Conclusion: 该框架支持具有明确延迟机制的可靠性导向多语言临床决策支持。领域自适应方法与选择性验证机制的结合能够有效处理多语言骨科文本分类中的挑战，为低资源医疗环境提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multilingual orthopedic decision support remains challenging in low-resource healthcare settings, where clinical narratives contain specialized terminology, mixed scripts, incomplete evidence, label imbalance and language-dependent documentation patterns. This article presents a reliability-oriented framework for classifying free-text orthopedic notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We compare task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, a task-fine-tuned DistilBERT baseline, zero-shot instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) and a domain-adaptive encoder, IndicBERT-HPA. IndicBERT-HPA augments IndicBERT with language-aware orthopedic adapter heads to support clinically relevant multilingual representation learning. Evaluation extends beyond aggregate accuracy to per-class performance, ROC-AUC, AUPRC, expected calibration error, cross-language stability and robustness under controlled balanced and natural-prevalence distributions. The evaluated zero-shot LLMs remain substantially less effective than task-adapted encoders for closed-set classification, with language-dependent instability. Under natural clinical prevalence, IndicBERT-HPA achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching an averaged Macro-F1 of 0.8792, Macro-AUROC of 0.894 and AUPRC of 0.902. We further implement a deterministic selective-verification layer combining confidence gating, evidence-consistency checking and language-risk screening. On a randomly selected held-out 5,000-record subset, it achieves 84.4% selective accuracy and 0.76 selective Macro-F1 at 72.3% coverage, compared with 71.5% accuracy and 0.65 Macro-F1 for accept-all prediction. These results support reliability-oriented multilingual clinical decision support with explicit deferral.

</details>


### [181] [UniAudio-Token: Empowering Semantic Speech Tokenizers with General Audio Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31521)
*Yuhan Song,Linhao Zhang,Aiwei Liu,Chuhan Wu,Sijun Zhang,Wei Jia,Yuan Liu,Houfeng Wang,Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniAudio-Token是一个增强语义语音tokenizer的框架，通过在保留语音能力的同时增强音频感知能力，通过语义-声学基元和语义-声学均衡两种创新来解决传统tokenizer的声学盲点问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语义语音tokenizer因其单码本设计和强语言对齐而广泛使用，但其过度关注语言抽象导致声学盲点，限制了在非语音任务中的应用。需要增强tokenizer的通用音频感知能力而不牺牲语音性能。

Method: 提出两种关键技术：1. 语义-声学基元(SAP)，将音频解构为语言内容、声乐属性和听觉场景基元，提供结构化监督；2. 语义-声学均衡(SAE)，引入内容感知的门控机制，从浅层自适应恢复细粒度声学细节。

Result: UniAudio-Token学习到全面的通用表示，同时保持高质量语音生成能力。当与下游LLMs集成时，在所有单码本baseline tokenizer上优于理解与生成任务，有效作为统一音频接口。

Conclusion: UniAudio-Token框架成功增强了语义tokenizer的通用音频感知能力，解决了声学盲点问题，成为高效的统一音频接口，代码和模型已公开发布。

Abstract: Semantic speech tokenizers have become a widely used interface for Audio-LLMs, owing to their compact single-codebook design and strong linguistic alignment. However, their focus on linguistic abstraction induces acoustic blindness, limiting their applicability beyond speech-centric tasks. We propose UniAudio-Token, a framework that empowers semantic tokenizers with general audio perception without compromising speech ability. Instead of altering the semantic paradigm, UniAudio-Token mitigates its information loss through two key innovations: (1) Semantic-Acoustic Primitives (SAP) provide structured supervision by decomposing audio into linguistic content, vocal attributes, and auditory-scene primitives; and (2) Semantic-Acoustic Equilibrium (SAE) introduces a content-aware gating mechanism that adaptively restores fine-grained acoustic details from shallow layers. Extensive evaluations show that UniAudio-Token learns comprehensive universal representations while preserving high-fidelity speech generation. When integrated with downstream LLMs, it outperforms all single-codebook baseline tokenizers on both understanding and generation tasks, effectively serving as a unified audio interface. We publicly release all our code, including training and inference scripts, together with the model checkpoints at https://github.com/Tencent/Universal_Audio_Tokenizer.

</details>


### [182] [Semantic Triplet Restoration: A Novel Protocol for Hierarchical Table Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31550)
*Yibin Zhao,Fangxin Shang,Dingrui Yang,Yuqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出语义三元组恢复（STR）协议，将表格数据重写为<项目路径, 特征路径, 值>的三元组形式，以减少标记开销并提高表格问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格问答系统通常使用HTML或Markdown作为中间表格表示，但这些布局导向的序列化方法引入了标记开销，并需要大型语言模型从行和列跨度中推断表头-单元格对齐关系。

Method: 提出了语义三元组恢复（STR）协议，将每个单元格重写为原子事实三元组<项目路径, 特征路径, 值>，其中项目路径指定行实体，特征路径指定分层属性，值包含单元格内容。还提出了TripletQL，一个轻量级的查询感知路由器，使用STR为每个问题选择适当的渲染或过滤后的三元组子集。

Result: 在四个中文和英文表格问答基准测试中，STR在减少输入标记的同时，匹配或改进了基于HTML的基线。对于较小的语言模型和较长的表格上下文，相对效益更加明显，表明显式语义表示在受约束的推理预算下特别有用。

Conclusion: 显式语义表示（如STR协议）在表格问答任务中优于传统的布局导向序列化方法，特别是在资源受限的场景下，能有效提高性能并减少计算开销。

Abstract: Table question answering requires models to recover semantic relations encoded implicitly by two-dimensional layout, merged cells, and hierarchical headers. Current pipelines typically use HTML or Markdown as intermediate table representations, but these layout-oriented serializations introduce markup overhead and require large language models to infer header-cell alignments from row and column spans. We propose Semantic Triplet Restoration (STR), a protocol that rewrites each cell as an atomic fact <item path, feature path, value>, where the item path specifies the row-wise entity, the feature path specifies the hierarchical attribute, and the value contains the cell content. We also present TripletQL, a lightweight query-aware router that uses STR to select an appropriate rendering or filtered subset of triplets for each question. Across four Chinese and English table-QA benchmarks, STR matches or improves upon HTML-based baselines while reducing input tokens. The relative benefit grows for smaller language models and longer table contexts, suggesting that explicit semantic representations are especially useful under constrained inference budgets. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Phoenix-ni/STR.git .

</details>


### [183] [What Am I Missing? Question-Answering as Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31561)
*Chu Fei Luo,Samuel Dahan,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出将提问作为推理时干预机制，通过学生-教师设置训练探测模型，发现提问生成过程中的自诊断信号能够预测最终正确性，但干预成功取决于模型的自一致性，存在检测与恢复之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs测试时推理机制尚未充分探索，即使从相同输入和部分解决方案，多次采样仍会产生不同答案。研究者希望通过提问干预来理解模型隐藏状态，并探索模型在不确定性下的自修正能力。

Method: 建立学生-教师设置，学生提问教师回答问题；训练探测模型分析学生在提问前后的隐藏状态；构建门控策略作为顺序决策问题，使用探测模型质量评分最大化正确性可能性。

Result: 探测模型预测能力表明提问生成过程中的自诊断信号比教师传递的信息更重要；干预成功高度依赖模型自一致性；门控策略能捕获模型正确性和不确定性，但干预对正确轨迹的损害概率与恢复错误轨迹的概率相当。

Conclusion: 提问作为推理时干预揭示了检测与恢复之间的差距，对LLMs在不确定性下的自我修正能力具有更广泛意义。模型能够诊断自身状态，但实际修正效果有限。

Abstract: Test-time reasoning has become a significant field of study since the introduction of chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanisms of this reasoning process are still under-explored -- from the same input prompt, and even the same partial solution, LLMs can produce varied answers if sampled multiple times. We propose to leverage question-asking as an inference-time intervention that articulates information about the model's hidden state. To achieve that, we present a student-teacher setting where a student asks questions to a teacher. We train a probe on the student's hidden state before and after asking a question and find it is predictive of the trajectory's final correctness, even before generating the teacher's answer. This suggests there is a meaningful signal from the self-diagnosis that occurs during question generation rather than information transfer from the teacher. We then frame question-asking as a sequential decision problem, using this probe as a quality score, and define a gating policy to ask questions that maximize likelihood of correctness. We find that the success of question-asking as an intervention is largely dependent on the model's self-consistency. Our empirical results show a gap between detection and recovery; while our gating policy captures model correctness and uncertainty, interventions are equally likely to harm correct trajectories as they are to recover incorrect ones. This gap between diagnosis and correction has broader implications on language models' capacity for self-refinement under uncertainty.

</details>


### [184] [Disagreeing Rationales: Rethinking Classification and Explainability Evaluation in Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31563)
*Benedetta Muscato,Beiduo Chen,Gizem Gezici,Barbara Plank,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个统一的监督框架，系统地评估在主观NLP任务（如仇恨言论检测）中不同标签和解释表示空间下的人类标注差异和解释变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在标注时的分歧是普遍存在的，但在解释（token-level人类理由）方面的变化研究较少。同时，如何在这种变化下最佳评估人类标签和理由，以及如何超越多数投票来聚合理由，这些问题尚未明确。理由可能为理解人类推理的丰富性提供额外见解，特别是在主观NLP任务中，推理风格、价值观和解释可能各不相同。
